- **rfc-promote**: 1) Ensure RFC status is `accepted` (or ask for confirmation). 2) Create `docs/90-adr/ADR-####-YYYY-MM-DD-&lt;slug&gt;.md` with MADR sections. 3) Set `derived_from: &lt;rfc-id&gt;` in ADR; add backlink to RFC if possible. 4) Suggest arc42 updates (§4/§5/§9).
